// Literal pools the SN64 compiler left in the text stream, between one function's `jr $ra`
// (plus its delay slot) and the next function's first instruction. Each was measured in the
// cartridge: the two words before every address below are `03E00008` and its delay slot, and
// the word after every block is the next function's opening instruction.
//
// They are declared here because they are data, not code. Without a type spimdisasm decodes
// words such as 0x0000000B as instructions (`movn`), which the period assembler does not
// accept; with it they are emitted as `.word` inside .text, which is where the cartridge
// keeps them. They must not become `bin`/`data` subsegments: a Splat `code` segment gathers
// every .data input section after every .text one, which would move these bytes out of the
// text stream and past the end of the segment's code.
D_8023EEDC = 0x8023EEDC; // type:u32 size:0x4 ROM 0x03FADC
D_80255C3C = 0x80255C3C; // type:u32 size:0x4 ROM 0x05683C
D_80276598 = 0x80276598; // type:u32 size:0x8 ROM 0x077198
D_802B243C = 0x802B243C; // type:u32 size:0x4 ROM 0x0B303C
D_802B24F8 = 0x802B24F8; // type:u32 size:0x8 ROM 0x0B30F8
D_802B83E4 = 0x802B83E4; // type:u32 size:0xC ROM 0x0B8FE4
D_802BCE4C = 0x802BCE4C; // type:u32 size:0x4 ROM 0x0BDA4C
D_802BD1A4 = 0x802BD1A4; // type:u32 size:0xC ROM 0x0BDDA4
D_8041F298 = 0x8041F298; // type:u32 size:0xD04 ROM 0x183298
D_80435CE4 = 0x80435CE4; // type:u32 size:0xC ROM 0x199CE4
D_804366C4 = 0x804366C4; // type:u32 size:0xC ROM 0x19A6C4
D_80439F0C = 0x80439F0C; // type:u32 size:0x4 ROM 0x19DF0C
D_80444024 = 0x80444024; // type:u32 size:0xC ROM 0x1A8024
D_80445844 = 0x80445844; // type:u32 size:0xC ROM 0x1A9844
D_804460E4 = 0x804460E4; // type:u32 size:0xC ROM 0x1AA0E4
D_80448E88 = 0x80448E88; // type:u32 size:0x8 ROM 0x1ACE88

// Names this cartridge uses for its own address while a shared source uses it for another.
// A placement is a linker-script assignment and an assignment overrides an object, so
// leaving these alone relocated this cartridge's own references without failing.
// 41 respelt, derived from this cartridge's own disassembly.
us_D_800C7E20 = 0x800C7E20; // absolute:True
us_D_800C8064 = 0x800C8064; // absolute:True
us_D_800C8068 = 0x800C8068; // absolute:True
us_D_800C87C8 = 0x800C87C8; // absolute:True
us_D_800C90D8 = 0x800C90D8; // absolute:True
us_D_800C90E0 = 0x800C90E0; // absolute:True
us_D_800C90E8 = 0x800C90E8; // absolute:True
us_D_800C90F0 = 0x800C90F0; // absolute:True
us_D_800C90F8 = 0x800C90F8; // absolute:True
HUD_SCORE_ANIM = 0x800C9350; // absolute:True
us_D_800D7990 = 0x800D7990; // absolute:True

// ---- shared-spelling disambiguations; maintained by hand, no command writes this file ----
// Nothing in the toolkit writes this file: every reference to [split].symbols is a read. The
// line here used to say this block was "rewritten on every run", which described a migration
// command that does not exist -- the rewriting has been done each time from scratch scripts a
// lane wrote for itself and then threw away. A sentence asserting behaviour nothing implements
// is the same defect as an address no other table can contradict, so it is corrected rather
// than left standing. Edits below are ordinary hand edits and are reviewed as such.
// Names this cartridge does not derive from its own addresses.
//
// One authored tree serves all five cartridges, and a name in it is derived from the reference
// cartridge's address. The same code is almost never at the same place, so the shared spelling is
// given this cartridge's address in the split below, and this cartridge's own interval at the
// literal address the spelling reads as takes the disambiguated spelling here -- the C can no
// longer be rewritten, so the inversion is forced. An interval whose derived name is the stem of a
// shared source it is not paired with is disambiguated for the same reason: left alone, that source
// would displace code it is not.

// 1915 shared name(s) at this cartridge's own address. Stated here because the
// splitter takes a function's name from this table and not from the subsegment's
// file name: renaming the subsegment alone left the interval's assembly defining
// the derived spelling while the shared C defined the shared one, so every caller
// in this cartridge's assembly reached the link undefined.
